Since the space is so small, the conversations of the other patrons took over the whole area.

entity_slug: lauria-s-by-the-beach
reviewer_name: Alyssa Campbell
rating: 3
body: We saw a TikTok recommendation for this place so we popped in for a late lunch. It is a very small dive bar with just a few tables inside and some high top seating around the patio. The food is standard bar food and it is not as good as it looks on their website. We ordered the baked meatballs, bruschetta, and a spicy bbq flatbread pizza. Everything was decent- it seemed mostly like a prep & heat situation as nothing was very seasoned. The service was excellent though. Since the space is so small, the conversations of the other patrons took over the whole area.
